- **Triangulation (WT → gold → Claude):** AGH1001 lesson 1, 1B — WT `[Interactive]` / `Drag and drop the products into the correct category.` / a two-column TABLE; gold `<div class="activity interactive" number="1B"><h3>Agricultural and horticultural products</h3><p>Drag and drop the products into the correct category.</p><div class="dragAndDrop …">`; Claude the r217 standalone box → the capture `⚙ INTERACTIVE (un-built) #2: interactive — Activity (inline)` with `<p>Drag and drop …</p>` as its first member, then the table. MXFL301 lesson 1 (a typed `[Activity 1E – self-marking drag and drop]` box, now r365's `<h3>`) — the instruction paragraph after the tag still inside the capture; the gold `<h3>` + `<p>` + widget. CEDT104 (dropDown) — the same.
- **Cause:** `InteractiveScanner.#swallowMembers` walks the black run after the tag into `bundle.memberItems` (a member — right for a carousel's captions, wrong for a drag-and-drop's instruction); the r362 owner form only frees the prose BEFORE the tag (`activityLeadItems`). The converter's `#interactivePlaceholder` builds the capture live from `bundle.memberItems` at emission time, so the fix can pull the lead out just before it.
- **Mechanism (ENGINE + data):** `Emit_Templates.activity_wrapper.lead_free_after_tag` {enabled, env `LEADFREE_OFF`, types_standalone, types_owned, exclude_code_prefixes [SSCI, SSFUN, SSOG, ARFUN], require_remaining_member true}: in the bundle-owner path, after the owner's lead renders and before the placeholder, for a box (standalone or owned) whose `bundle.type` is in the list for its owner kind: the consecutive `black` members after the opener tag (before the first table / list / tag) render as free paragraphs (`ListsAndRuns.renderBlackText`, the r362 lead's own renderer) inside the box, are marked `consumedBy = "lead-free"` and leave `bundle.memberItems`, so the capture, the build (`InteractiveBuilder.Build` reads the members) and the `.txt` hand-off never repeat them; at least one non-black member must remain (a table / list / tag) or nothing moves; never on reoMode pages or in the MTK quiz shell. OFF = byte-identical.
- **Expected effect:** ≈ 400 captures on ≈ 260 pages lose their leading paragraph(s) to the box (the skeleton pairs the gold's free `p`); the built dragAndDrop / dropDown / reorder widgets in the family keep their tables (the builders read the table, not the lead — `#dragAndDrop` line 299); dips expected where the gold folds the lead into its own widget (the 0.31 minority — named per type); every other page byte-identical. Proof: the in-memory probe over all 416; a FULL regeneration is the honest scope (7 widget families across 219 modules — the §0a family union is most of the corpus, and the ledger's scoped counter would jump to 2 of 8); gates including the dragAndDrop / dropDown verifiers over their whole families; finalise r366 = build 260619.37.

- **KB (§1b level 1):** 01F `activity_heading` → `<h3>Activity heading text</h3>` within the activity; the writer's instruction paragraph is activity-level prose; the widget follows. Gold 0.83–0.92 on the tag forms.
- **Mechanism (ENGINE + data, the same seam; data `Interactive_Boundary_ChildTag_Bank._meta.opener_rule.unclassified_activity_lead.heading_led_owner` {enabled, env `UNCLASSHEAD_OFF`, member_form_tags [h2, h3, h4, h5] — the tags `#swallowMembers` actually terminates on}):** the guard keeps the OLD member form only when the lead's opening heading tag is one of `member_form_tags` (the r362-measured MXFUN01 / MXFL203 / AGH1008 shape, byte-identical); a lead opening with `activity heading` / `heading` / `h1` / `h6` takes the OWNER form — `bundle.activityOwner` = the opener, `activityLeadItems` = the heading item + the prose up to the first table, members from that table. ContentConverter's existing lead rendering already promotes a heading-tag lead item to the `<h3>` (`["h1","h2","h3","h4","h5","heading","activity heading"]` in `addLead`). OFF = r362's guard = byte-identical.
- **Expected effect:** ≈ 70–150 boxes on ≈ 55–110 pages gain the gold's `h3` (+ `p` / `ul`) before the capture; the skeleton pairs those lines (r362's +0.173pp came from ≈ 290 pages). Proof: the in-memory probe over all 416 (`UNCLASSHEAD_OFF=1` = disk 2110 / 2110; ON = the changed set), the per-page skeleton delta on the changed pages against the r362 pages before any regeneration; dips named. Regeneration: the changed modules + the §0b family (every module whose WT carries a heading-tag-led bare opener — the probe's 721-box population, ≈ 200 modules) → a FULL regeneration is simplest (ledger counter 0, 8 of headroom; the corpus-wide class of a scanner seam).

- **Cause (InteractiveScanner, the "activity WITHOUT a widget keyword but WITH a data table" path, r-old):** the opener item itself is collected as the bundle's FIRST member ("its blackAfter carries the activity title / instructions") and `#swallowMembers` runs from the next item, so the whole activity becomes the placeholder box. The NORMAL path (a real interactive tag inside an activity) does it the gold's way: the lookback records the `[Activity N]` item as `bundle.activityOwner` and the items between it and the widget tag as `bundle.activityLeadItems`; ContentConverter renders the owner's blackAfter as the box's `<h3>` title, the lead items as prose, and only then the widget box.

The marking loop (opener … endIndex consumed) and the converter's existing owner rendering do the rest: `<h3>` title, the lead prose, the placeholder box with the tables. OFF = the opener collected as before = byte-identical.
- **Expected effect:** ≈ 900 boxes on ≈ 500 pages gain the gold's `h3` (+ the instruction `p`) before the capture — the skeleton pairs those lines; the miner's #542 / #535 / #533 shrink. In-memory probe over all 416 → the changed set; per-page skeleton delta on the changed pages before the full regeneration; named dips if any (a box whose gold has no h3 — the "OTHER" residue — would be unchanged since the owner form only adds the title the opener carries).

OFF = byte-identical (the flag never set, the detector returns off).

Never merge into one'; constraint 79 / 02C line 62: a lesson page's second h1 appears where the writer gave THAT lesson its own bilingual name, 'split by the same TITLE BAR parsing rule'; 01A 'the parts are emitted in the order the writer wrote them' (overview) and D10-2 (Chris: English first for a Standard lesson pair — ANZH104's Māori-first gold order becomes a NAMED order override, as ANZH105 / HIS1006 were at r345). ## Session 19 · Round 0c (THE DIFF MINER — LOOP §1d, Chris's 17 Sept amendment) — BUILT, RUN ON THE CURRENT CORPUS, COMMITTED (no converter output changes)
- **What was built:** `reference/tests/_diff_miner.py` (selftest: LIVENESS + FACTS + DERIVABLE + DETECTION, PASS). For every paired page of the skeleton gate's OWN population (`_corpus.gate_mods` + `_discrepancy_audit.pairs` + the acks / glossary / references filter — 1955 pairs / 405 modules, 0 parse errors) it builds the gold and Claude SCAFFOLD skeletons with the gate's own tree, labels, widget collapse and note skip — one line per element (the gate's `N× repeated` collapse is a scoring convenience; a mined line must be quotable) with its TEXT and its REGION — aligns them with the gate's own difflib (autojunk=False) and tags every differing line: template / Legacy-vs-Refresh / subject / series / overview-vs-lesson; REGION (module-code, title, header, module-menu, crumbs, phases-nav, activity, body, footer, acks); ROLE (parent › element, a lone inline wrapper folded into its parent so the gold's `h4>span` vs Claude's `h5` is ONE class); DIRECTION (MISSING / EXTRA / SUBSTITUTED / MOVED; the descendants of a one-sided subtree ride with its top line); DERIVABLE (the gold text under `anchor_compare._phrase_present`, the round-110 tolerance, via `_measure_ceiling.has_source`; structure-only differences always derivable). Classes are keyed (region, parent, gold form, Claude form, direction) with pages / modules / per-template / per-subject / per-era / per-page-type counts, a MULTIPLICITY-AWARE gold consensus (a MISSING class on pages where the gold carries K of the element asks how many gold pages carry ≥ K — a second title h1 is a convention only where second h1s are; an EXTRA class asks how many gold pages carry fewer than Claude's K; the denominator is the CONTAINING region's gold pages, so a page without the chip is still in the module-code denominator), the derivable share, up to three KB lines (term + token grep), the §1b authority and three example modules with the WT / gold / Claude lines quoted. Two alignment-free instruments ride alongside: the CHROME FACT census (per page, the header's chip / chip form / head-buttons / menu-content / title-h1 count and the footer's ul class / link set / link order / inside-body, plus crumbs / phases presence — MISSING / EXTRA per fact with the gold share per group) and the §1d item-4 COMPLETENESS census for the module menu / crumbs / phases nav / footer (the gold's text-bearing items whose text is in the WT vs what Claude rendered). Output: `outputs/_diff_miner.json` (42 MB — every class, group and page record; not mirrored) and **`DIFF_QUEUE.md`** at the folder root (mirrored). 65 s for the whole corpus under WSL — no sharding needed.
- **Two traps found and closed while building it:** `outputs/_corpus.py` is a STALE June copy (no `gate_mods`) that shadows the gate's helper when `outputs/` precedes `reference/tests/` on `sys.path` — the miner puts the tests dir first; and native Windows Python cannot open the `CONVERTER_V2/data` symlink (EINVAL) and prints cp1252 — every gate tool runs under WSL, as the Environment section says.

## Session 16 · Round 1 PICK (engine r356 — NO built widget discards a member it never READ: the r353 members rule made EXACT by read-tracking) — written before any code, 2026-09-17 13:20 NZST
- **Why this is a class ≥ 20 pages (the §3 step-1 question the session-15 stop left open):** the r353 class — a built widget silently drops a writer's member paragraph — was neither declined (no share test failed) nor blocked (no question for Chris); it was SHIPPED OFF because a text-presence test at the seam cannot know what a builder consumed. The exact design was queued as "each builder reports its consumed members". This session found a cheaper exact signal that needs NO per-builder edit: **a member the builder never READ cannot be in the build.** `outputs/_measure_r356_consumed.cjs` wraps `InteractiveBuilder.Build` and, for the build's duration only, replaces every `memberItem` with a Proxy that records a read of `text` / `blackAfter` / `block` / `nestedBundle` and records every `bundle.tables` read; the originals are restored before the caller sees the bundle. Over all 416 modules (2511 built bundles; `_r356_consumed.log`, `_r356_consumed.json`, `_r356_consumed_detail.log`): **30 built widgets on 26 pages / 26 modules drop a never-read prose member (53 text runs) — flipCard 22 builds / 18 pages (32 runs: the TABLE-form paths `#flipCardDialects` / `#flipCardCards` read `bundle.tables` alone, every black / `[body]` member before or after the card table is dropped), carousel 4 / 4 (a `[body]` after the slide table on BLL117 / 127 / 137 / 157 — the r352 members discipline covers black lines, not the `[body]` tag's words), accordion 2 / 2 (XDLS906's un-parsed bracket line, BLL246's word-select story lines), speechBubble 1, hintSlider 1.** Per template family: Standard 20 pages, Fundamentals 3, Inquiry 3. Nothing per-builder about the rule: one seam, one classification, the builders untouched.
- **The gold (authority level 3, the share test):** the gold keeps the lost text as free body (`<p>` / `<li>`) on **41 of 53 runs (0.77) and 17 of 26 pages (0.65) — Standard 12/20 = 0.60, Fundamentals 3/3, Inquiry 2/3** (the "drops" are a Claude page with no gold pair — CEDO301 6.0 — a human's commented-out paragraph — AGH1006 "Grafting…" — and two label lines); ≥ 0.60 in every family, and the "never lose the writer's words" policy (r351 / r352 / r354) applies regardless.
- **Triangulation:** (i) **AGH1009 1.0 #1** (flipCard: a 2-column card table + two trailing black paragraphs "So, with our current global demands for food…" / "This video gives a local example of kaitiakitanga…" + a `[button]`): gold = the cards, then both paragraphs as `<p>`; Claude = the cards and nothing after. (ii) **TWHK901 0.0 #6 / #9 / #12** (three flipCards, each followed by 1–3 black paragraphs "We have a responsibility to honour the principles…", "As you can see, waste occurs in every area…"): gold `<p>` each; Claude 0 of 6 paragraphs on the page. (iii) **CHFUN05 0.0 #30** (`[flip card]` + `[body]` "Flip the card to see the sentence in English." + the table): gold keeps the sentence before the cards; Claude drops it.
- **The false-consumption check (an untouched member whose words ARE on the page):** 11 sites, all TEDC402 / CEDO202 — nine are iStock reference-title lines ("Young Beautiful Student Woman Set Stock Illustration - Download Image Now…", the r240 class, on the page as the image's alt / acks) and one is a sentence the writer typed twice; so the exact rule keeps two guards from r353: the built html's own text (shingle test) and a `reference_title_pattern` (stock-site titles, "– YouTube" tails) → never learner prose.
- **Strict-mode forecast (an untouched member the seam cannot render):** 17 flipCard bundles — 15 untouched `[image]` / `[video]` members whose URL is not in the build (TEDC402 ×3, SSOG103 ×2, MXFL301, ANZH205, TEFUN08, XGF9002, ENGJ202, TEDC401, XGF9006), 6 `[button]` members (5 = the go-journal button the caller already emits — consumed; AGH1009's "Complete activity 1A in your journal" is not), 1 stray `[accordion]` tag. An untouched `[image]` member renders through the seam's `renderImage` (the tabs / r352 precedent — MediaBuilder.image, the page's own image emitter) before / after the widget; a video / audio / other tag / heading / table / nested member → the build DECLINES (the honest box keeps everything; the r352 precedent — expected ≈ 6–8 reverts, named).
- **Target form:** the widget keeps its build byte-for-byte; every member the builder never read that is prose (a black paragraph, a `[body]` / bracket-less line with ≥ 3 words) renders through the free-body emitter as ordinary `<p>` / `<ul>` BEFORE the widget when it precedes the first consumed member and AFTER it when it follows the last; an untouched `[image]` member renders through `renderImage`; prose BETWEEN consumed members longer than the short-line allowance, or any other untouched member, declines the build. The invocation tag (k = 0, directive INTERACTIVE) is neither consumed nor prose — r354's note owns its words.
- **Mechanism (one seam, one flag):** `InteractiveBuilder.Build` installs the read tracker around the type dispatch (`#trackMembers` — the probe's proxies; restored in `finally`) when `_members_rule.enabled` and `consumption: "read"`; `#withMembers` takes the tracker: a member READ by the builder (or a table read through `bundle.tables`) is CONSUMED, full stop; an unread member is classified as r353 did but with the text-presence test demoted to a guard (unread + its words in the built html → consumed; unread + reference-title → skip), unread `[image]` → `renderImage`, the rest as r353's strict mode. Data `Emit_Templates.interactive_builders._members_rule` {enabled: true, consumption: "read", reference_title_pattern, image_member_render: true; the r353 keys kept}; env `WIDGETMEMBERS_OFF` = the r354 corpus byte-for-byte (the 416-module OFF probe proves it). No widget markup changes; `exclude_types` keeps dragAndDrop on its own r351 rule.
- **Authority:** the gold (level 3 — ≥ 0.60 in every family) + the "never lose the writer's words / never half-build" policy (r351 / r352 / r354, D10-3's own words). **Expected gates:** skeleton neutral-to-up (restored `<p>` lines the gold has on 17 pages; a few reverts — named with companions), cs exact up, missing / body may move on the reverts — named; every widget verifier holds (a build is byte-identical or reverts to the box); the read census after the round must read 0 lossy-prose bundles; the r355 text-loss census must show flipCard lossy builds falling (53 → ≈ 30) and no other type rising; the probe's classify must show 0 duplicated text runs. Plateau window: predicts a skeleton move (counts).
- **Regeneration (REGENERATE CORPUS, §0b):** every widget family — a FULL regeneration (the r354 runner).

## Session 15 · Round 5 PICK (measurement-tool round r355 — the skeleton scorer's autojunk cliff) — written before any code, 2026-09-17 12:50 NZST
- **Why now (§3 step 6 / the r315 precedent):** r352 named the artefact once — CHFUN06 0.0 fell 56.1 → 25.5 % when five restored `<p>` (the gold has them) pushed its skeleton from 198 to 203 lines. The cause is proven: `difflib.SequenceMatcher`'s AUTOJUNK heuristic (on by default) marks any line occurring in more than 1 % of a sequence of 200+ items as junk and never matches on it — and a skeleton is MADE of such lines ("p", "WIDGET", "div.row"). A trial scorer with `autojunk=False` on the r354 corpus (`_r355_skeleton_noautojunk.py` → `_r355_sk_noautojunk.{json,log}`): **128 pages change, 126 UP** (BLL170 0.0 14.1 → 58.2, TEFUN02 13.4 → 57.1, BLL210 14.3 → 54.8, BLL140 16.2 → 56.2, TEFUN04 14.3 → 53.3, CEDO502 1.1 18.5 → 53.3 — every long single-page Fundamentals / BLL module the dashboard has listed as a "worst page" for months), 2 down by < 1pp; **the corpus mean 50.9646 → 51.9788 % (+1.01pp), ≥ 50 % 1073 → 1093, ≥ 75 / ≥ 90 unchanged.** A measurement defect this size cannot wait for a third naming: the queue is otherwise exhausted at the floor (every remaining text-loss shape < 20 sites; the D10-3 kickoffs exhausted; no KB row; no structural class since r341), so the tooling round is the next PICK.
- **Class:** gate-configuration / measurement tool — `_skeleton_compare.py` scores with `autojunk=False` (`SKAUTOJUNK=1` restores the old numbers for a one-off comparison); every baseline that carries a skeleton number is RE-ESTABLISHED on the same corpus (`gate_baseline.json`, CLAUDE.md §9 / §14, the fast-loop snapshot, the loop's "% of achievable"), the +1.01pp recorded as an INSTRUMENT correction, never a gain. No engine or data change; no regeneration; every other gate untouched. Gate-neutral by design (neither counts toward the plateau window nor resets it).
- **Proof:** the scorer's `--selftest` GREEN; the full corpus scored with the new instrument = the trial numbers exactly; the ceiling instrument (`_measure_ceiling.py`) does not use SequenceMatcher (unchanged, 91.9 %); `% of achievable` = 51.98 / 91.9 = 56.6 % (was 55.5 %).

## Session 15 · Round 4 PICK (engine r354 — the words on a widget tag's own line become the Writers Note) — written before any code, 2026-09-17 11:40 NZST
- **Class:** the exact sub-class the r353 measurement isolated — **the words a writer types on the widget invocation tag's OWN line** ("[flip card] Please present each word on a flip card so that one word is on each side", "[drag and drop] answers are placed in the correct columns, please place outside the box", "[carousel] Click on the slide to see examples of noun 和 hé noun", "[speech bubble] example of how to display this") — LOST on every built widget of every type: the builders read the tag as the invocation and never the words beside it. Measured over all 416 (`_measure_r354_tagwords.cjs` → `_r354_tagwords.json`, `_r354_tagwords.log`): **193 built widgets — flipCard 51 (42 pages / 37 modules), carousel 37 (37 / 32), dragAndDrop 33 (25 / 25 — the words sit in the tag's own text, not its blackAfter, so the r351 paragraph never saw them), speechBubble 33 (16 / 10), modal 15, clickDrop 7, tabs 6, accordion 5, hintSlider 5, dropDown 1, multiChoiceQuiz 1.** The gold keeps them as learner text on only 14 / 51 flipCard, 4 / 37 carousel, 4 / 15 modal, 0 / 33 dragAndDrop and speechBubble lines — they are MOSTLY developer instructions ("please…", "can we…", "if possible…").
- **Triangulation:** (i) CHFUN05 0.0 #4 (flipCard) "[flip card] Click on the card to see examples of noun or pronoun 呢 in English" — gold `<p>` before the cards, Claude nothing; (ii) BLL147 1.0 (dragAndDrop) "[drag and drop] answers are placed in the correct columns, please place outside the box" — a developer instruction, gold nothing, Claude nothing; (iii) ENGI401 1.0 (carousel) "[carousel] underneath each image have a hintslider function if not able…" — a developer instruction, lost.
- **Target form:** the r214 instruction-member class — the red Writers Note after the widget (`<p class="cv2-note">Writers Note: …</p>`), where the developer decides whether it is learner text (the 14 CHFUN05-type lines) or a request; never guessed into learner prose, never a reason to decline the build. Unless the build's own visible text already carries the words (a heading the builder made of them, the r351 paragraph) or a note already says them; red runs on the line are already notes and are not doubled.
- **Mechanism (one seam, one flag):** `InteractiveBuilder.Build` → `#tagWordsNote({ bundle, html, templates })` after every successful build (dragAndDrop included): the invocation tag's `text` after the bracket + its `blackAfter`, red runs and bracket tokens stripped, ≥ min_words words, 4-word-shingle presence test against the built html's text + alt / title, de-duplicated against `bundle.instructions` → pushed to `bundle.instructions` (the caller emits every instruction as the red note after the widget). Data `Emit_Templates.interactive_builders._tag_words_note` {enabled, env `TAGWORDSNOTE_OFF`, min_words 3}. No widget markup changes; a build never reverts. `TAGWORDSNOTE_OFF=1` = the r353 corpus byte-for-byte.
- **Authority:** the r214 / r350 instruction-member precedent (a writer's note inside a widget is surfaced, not lost) + the "never lose the writer's words" policy; the gold's own placement (learner text on ≈ 10 %) is the developer's call from the note. **Expected gates:** gate-neutral by design on the skeleton (a red note is chrome the scorer excludes) and every structural gate; the widget verifiers unchanged (no markup edit); the text-loss census after the round must show the tag-words count falling to 0 and the notes present. The plateau window: gate-neutral by design — neither counts nor resets.
- **Regeneration (REGENERATE CORPUS, §0b):** every widget family — a FULL regeneration.

## Session 15 · Round 3 (engine r353 — the generic members rule) — BUILT, MEASURED, SHIPPED OFF (the corpus is byte-identical to r352)
- **Closed 2026-09-17 ≈11:32 NZST (session 15).** AppVersion 260619.24, changelog entry r353, CLAUDE.md §11 / §14, `gate_baseline.json` `_meta` only; `Emit_Templates.interactive_builders._members_rule.enabled: false`; the flag-off probe = disk 2110/2110; a FULL regeneration with the flag off → manifest IDENTICAL, 0 stale. No gate re-run (an identical corpus); every r352 baseline stands. Ship ledger unchanged (counter 0).
- **What the probe measured (`_r353_probe_classify.log`, `_r353_dupcheck.log`, `_r353_wm_debug{,2}.log`):** STRICT mode (an un-renderable un-consumed member declines the build) — 291 pages / 188 modules, **475 built widgets revert to the box on 150 pages** (flipCard 149, clickDrop 96, accordion 67, modal 65, dropDown 45, carousel 41, tabs 12), most for reasons the seam cannot verify (a builder's own transformation of a media URL, a title it drops by rule, a same-type sub-tag's short words) with real losses mixed in (BLL210 0.0 #21's second video, dropped because a channel link rides its title); PROSE-ONLY mode — 160 pages / 121 modules, 228 `<p>` restored, 0 reverts, **but duplicated text on 18 pages** (HPRE203 5.0's flipCard rows rendered again, CEDO301 6.0, ENFUN01 0.0, XMES103 1.0) and writer notes surfaced as prose ("x 4 slides"). Five refinements (media-tag words = reference titles, go-journal buttons, iStock ids / URL slugs, short-between skip, the prose-only mode) did not make it safe: **a text-presence test at the seam cannot know what a builder consumed.**
- **Not BLOCKED on Chris — a design finding.** The next design: each builder REPORTS the members it used (`bundle._consumedMembers` — one seam, ten builders, 2–3 engine rounds), after which the rule is exact; until then the losses are closed builder by builder where the authoring shape is exact: (1) flipCard — the card table's first row is the writer's instruction sentence dropped as a "label row" (41 builds / ≈ 35 pages; CHFUN05 "Click on the card to see examples…"); (2) modal — the caption under `[modal] label` + `[image]` / `[video]` (MXFL101 ×10, BLL1xx); (3) accordion — the XDLS `[accordion] [H2] [body] [video]` body (12); (4) the carousel in-table title row (OSGM301 1.0 = the gold's `<h3>`; 44) and the video carousels' reference titles (80 — mostly the r240 reference-title class, fine to drop; the real losses are videos dropped by the title-link confusion, BLL210). The code stays behind the flag.
- **Lessons for the tools:** (1) a probe classify must check DUPLICATED text as well as lost text — the prose-only mode looked clean by every other number; (2) `const html` in a builder → a reassignment throws "Assignment to constant variable" inside Build's catch and looks like a decline — read the build NOTES (`run.notes`) in every probe, not just the output; (3) the Windows Python heredoc path is unusable for engine patches (tabs / backslashes / `\u{…}` are mangled) — every engine or data edit goes through a scratchpad script written with the editor and run as a file.

## Session 15 · Round 3 PICK (engine r353 — NO built widget discards the writer's words: the generic members rule at the Build seam) — written before any code, 2026-09-17 11:05 NZST
- **Class:** the text-loss census residue after r352 (`_r352_widgetloss_post.log`, confirmed against the gold in `_r353_losses_by_type.log`): **flipCard 68 lossy builds / 54 pages / 43 modules, modal 45 / 36 / 23, accordion 35 / 34 / 23, clickDrop 21 / 19 / 17, dropDown 16 / 16 / 12, carousel residue 110 / 93** — and for every type the gold keeps the lost text mostly as ordinary `<p>` paragraphs (flipCard 58 runs, carousel 103, modal 55, accordion 16, dropDown 9 + 10 `<li>`). Decomposed by member shape: flipCard — 41 of 68 lose text INSIDE the card table (the r-era `#looksLikeFlipHeader` drops a first row that is really the writer's instruction sentence: CHFUN05 "Click on the card to see examples of noun or pronoun", "Flip the card to see the examples in English"), 10 lose a trailing black paragraph, 4 a leading one; modal — the `[modal] label` + `[image]` / `[video]` + black-caption form loses the caption / the "Go to journal" line (MXFL101 ×10, BLL1xx ×8); accordion — the XDLS `[accordion] [H2] [body] [video]` form loses the body text (12) and 3 lose in-table text; clickDrop — 15 in-table (MXFUN01's image-reference lines = the r240 class, mostly fine to drop) + leading `[body]` / `[h3]` prose (4); dropDown — MXFL302's calculation paragraphs.
- **Triangulation:** (i) **AGH1009 1.0 #1** (flipCard, a 2-column card table + a trailing black paragraph "So with our current global demands for food, the dwindling levels of arable land…" + a `[button]`): gold = the flip cards, then that paragraph as `<p>` (confirmed absent from Claude's page, present in the gold); (ii) **CHFUN05 0.0 #4** (flipCard, the table's first row "Click on the card to see examples of noun or pronoun" = the writer's instruction; the builder reads it as a label row and drops it): gold keeps the sentence; (iii) **ENGI103 2.0 #4** (modal `[modal]` + `[body]` "This story shows how proud the boys are of their culture…"): gold keeps the sentence as `<p>`; Claude's pop-out drops it.
- **Target form:** the widget keeps its build; every member paragraph the build did not consume renders as an ordinary paragraph BEFORE the widget (if it precedes the widget's consumed content) or AFTER it (if it follows) through the free-body block emitter — the gold's `<p>`; a first table row that is a SENTENCE (a cell of ≥ 5 words), not a label pair (`Front | Back`, `Image | Text`), renders as a paragraph before the widget; anything the seam cannot render (a heading, a media tag whose URL is not in the build, another widget's tag, prose BETWEEN consumed members, a table the build ignored) → the build DECLINES and the hand-off box keeps every member (never half-build).
- **Mechanism (one seam, one flag):** `InteractiveBuilder.Build` — after the type dispatch returns html for ANY type except dragAndDrop (its own r351 rule) — `#withMembers({ bundle, html, renderBlock, tpl: templates._members_rule })`: (1) `consumed` = the normalised visible text of the built html plus its alt / title attributes and the bundle's instruction notes; (2) each member is CONSUMED when ≥ consumed_ratio (0.5) of its 4-word shingles appear in `consumed` (a member of ≤ 6 words: its whole text), a table when any sentence cell is consumed or it holds no sentence, a media tag when its URL / video id appears; (3) un-consumed prose (black, `[body]` with words, the invocation tag's own words, a sentence row of an otherwise-consumed table) renders via `renderBlock` before / after the widget by position; prose between two consumed members → decline; any other un-consumed member → decline. Data `Emit_Templates.interactive_builders._members_rule` {enabled, env `WIDGETMEMBERS_OFF`, exclude_types ["dragAndDrop"], min_words 3, shingle 4, consumed_ratio 0.5, sentence_row_words 5, note_cue_pattern}. `WIDGETMEMBERS_OFF=1` = the r352 corpus byte-for-byte (the 416-module probe proves it; the r351 / r352 rules stay as they are and their members read as consumed).
- **Authority:** the gold (level 3 — the lost runs are `<p>` in the gold across every type) + the "never half-build" policy (D10-3's own words) + the r351 / r352 precedents. **Expected gates:** skeleton neutral-to-up (restored `<p>` lines match the gold; some reverts), cs exact up; body / missing may move on the reverts — named; every widget verifier holds (a build either keeps its html byte-identical or reverts to the box — the rule never edits the widget's own markup); the text-loss census after the round must show every type's lossy count falling and NO duplicated text run on any changed page (the probe's classify checks duplicates).
- **Regeneration (REGENERATE CORPUS, §0b):** every widget family — a FULL regeneration (the r352 runner).

A strict "tag + table only" fence would have left the column shape at **13 sites / 10 pages** (under the floor) and the loss in the 21 shipped builds untouched. **The round therefore becomes the members class — "a built dragAndDrop never discards its bundle's other members" (21 shipped sites ≥ the floor; the widget's own A1 territory) — with the column shape riding along on the same builder / verifier / regeneration (15 sites).** The rule (`interactive_builders.dragAndDrop.members`, env `DDMEMBERS_OFF`, applied after every built form in `InteractiveBuilder.Build`): the table = the widget; a black paragraph renders in place through the free-body paragraph emitter (`renderBlock`, the accordion rich-panel precedent) before or after the widget as the writer placed it; a `[body]` tag with words is a paragraph; the words riding the invocation tag's own line render as a paragraph BEFORE the widget (the safe content-preserving form — the activity's h3 is the r334 mechanism's business); an instruction-class tag is already the red Writers Note; a `[button]` whose label is the 03B row's own (undo / check / check answers / reset) is satisfied by the row the build emits; a `[body]` or tag-line text opening with a developer cue (CS: / Dev: / Note: / NB) is a note → decline; ANYTHING ELSE ([button] "Go to journal" / "Quiz" / "Weblink" / "Complete activity", [answer], [audio], [image], [activity], a nested widget) cannot be rendered from inside the widget seam → the build is DECLINED and the hand-off box keeps every member (never half-build). **Final probe (`_r351_probe_classify.log`, OFF = `DDCOLUMN_OFF=1 DDMEMBERS_OFF=1` = disk 2110 / 2110; ON = 24 pages / 22 modules):** 15 column sorts built (BLL220 ×2, BLL230 ×3, SSOG301 4.0 ×2, BLL147, BLL253, ENFUN08, ENGI203, ENGI302, ENGI401, ENGJ202, ENGR201), 8 shipped builds regain their lost writer text (BLL112, CHFUN05, ENGC301, ENGS201, OSGM101, PHE1005, SSOG301 6.0, TEFUN06), 5 shipped builds REVERT to the hand-off box because a member cannot be rendered (AGH1001's [Answer guide] + Weblink [Button], AGH1005's "Complete activity" [Button], BLL122's [Insert audio 2], ENGS302's [Table of themes…] activity tag, TEFUN04 #8's [Button] Quiz — each one a silent content loss today), 0 pages lose text. **The dashboard "moved" test will read 805 → ≈ 795 (−15 built, +5 honest reverts) — NOT the ≥ 20 line; reported as-is, with the 13 pages whose lost text this round restores or surfaces.** The `DDCOLUMN_OFF` toggle alone = the members rule on the r69 / r350 forms only; both OFF = the r350 corpus.

- **Target form (KB 03B "Standard Layout — With images", level 1):** `<div class="dragAndDrop images" layout="standard">` → `col-7 questionContainer` (one `<div class="question"><p>TEXT</p></div>` per row — the TEXT side is ALWAYS the fixed question side, whichever column the writer put it in: the KB's usability rule "images in the questionContainer stretch vertically") → `col-5 ddContainer` (`dropContainer` one `drop option=N` per row; `dragContainer` one `<div class="drag" option="N"><img class="img-fluid margB0" …></div>` per row, `option` = the 1-based row index = the answer key) → the KB button row `reset / undo hidden / checkAnswer hidden`. The image: iStock id → `images/iStock-{id}.jpg`, any other host → the r278 URL-slug placeholder filename (the accordion / banner / speech-bubble convention); Mode P/D through the r240 `FinishImg` rider (alt from the verified acks map else the URL-slug title — the gold's BLL146 alt IS the iStock title); NO `loading="lazy"` (c83 — the r318 `formatter.lazy_free_hosts` post-pass already strips inside `dragAndDrop`; the verifier proves it). The KB's optional `autoCheck` / `noShuffle` / `noBG` modifiers are the developer's editorial choice — not emitted (no writer signal). **Ride-along, same round, its own flag:** the KB button row on EVERY built standard D&D (the r69 text form never emitted it; the gold carries it on 473 / 517 = 0.92 of its standard D&Ds — level 1 and level 3 agree) — `interactive_builders.dragAndDrop.button_row` {enabled, env `DDBUTTONS_OFF`}; it touches the 58 working-half builds, which the §0b family regeneration covers anyway.
- **Mechanism:** `InteractiveBuilder.#dragAndDrop` keeps its r69 text form byte-for-byte (it bails on any URL); the dispatch gains `?? #dragAndDropImages(...)` (the r276 order — the narrow branch first, the new form only where the old returned null, so the working half is unchanged BY CONSTRUCTION apart from the button row). Data `interactive_builders.dragAndDrop.images` {enabled, open / mid / drag / image_mode_D / image_mode_P / image_mode_P_comment, image_tag_prefix, min_rows} + env `DDIMAGES_OFF`. Never half-build: any row that is not exactly image-cell + text-cell, a text-cell red run, a ragged table, a second table, an extraType, a harvested media item, an empty label, a duplicate label (an ambiguous match) → null → the hand-off box as today.
- **The NEW protected verifier `_verify_dragdrop.cjs`** (BEFORE the build ships; `run_all_gates.sh` + the selftest INJECT spec): converts each module in memory (the batch path, Mode P), harvests every `<div class="dragAndDrop …" layout="standard">` on the ASSEMBLED pages, and per widget checks the 03B form: a `questionContainer` / `ddContainer` pair; `dropContainer` + `dragContainer` present; questions = drops = drags (standard); every `drag` `option` has a `drop` and vice-versa; the button row present; no raw `[tag]` in the visible text; NO `loading="lazy"` on any `<img>` inside; an `images` widget's every drag holds exactly one `<img>` (Mode-P placeholder + comment) and every question holds text; reports per module `widgets N (images M) / defect D`; protected criterion defect 0 (✓ at baseline / ✗ above — the r348 form). Selftest INJECT: an `option="9"` drag with no drop + a `loading="lazy"` on a drag image → defect must register.
- **Authority:** KB 03B (level 1) + Chris's D10-3 (A1); the gold's substitutions (BLL112 / ENFUN04 column forms, the BLL146 exact match) are recorded, never chased. **Expected gates:** skeleton — the 20 pages swap a hand-off box (a `table` inside the cv2 wrapper) for the widget's row / col chain: predicted a small POSITIVE move (the gold carries a `dragAndDrop` on every one of these pages; BLL146-type exact matches gain, column-form pages gain the shared `row` / `dragAndDrop` lines); the button row adds one `row` line to the 58 working-half sites where the gold has it 0.92 → predicted positive; every other gate holds. The plateau "moved" test for this build round = the dashboard's dragAndDrop *Still a box* count (824 → ≤ 803).
- **Regeneration (REGENERATE CORPUS, §0b):** the whole dragAndDrop tag family — every module carrying a `[drag and drop]`-family tag (`_r350_dd_modules.txt`: 297 modules with a table-carrying bundle; the family list from the batch planner ≈ 309) — the working half (58 builds gain the button row) and the broken half (21 sites build) together; probe all 416 first (`DDIMAGES_OFF=1 DDBUTTONS_OFF=1` = the disk corpus 2110 / 2110).

- **Pre-measurement (`outputs/_measure_r348_menulabels.py` → `_r348_menulabels.{json,log}`; 1,056 paired lesson pages with a menu on both sides; the r341 void-aware parser):**
  (a) **The year-level table does not exist in the data.** `Module_Structure_Index.json` `module_meta` carries subject / template_type / prefix / phase / series / dev_order — no year — and the code's level digit is NOT a year (digit 1 = NCEA1 AGH10 / HIS10 / PES10 = years 11+, all `You will show…`; the 1–10 series' digit 1–3 is MIXED at every digit: 1 → YWS 189 / I-can 112, 2 → 88 / 73, 3 → 134 / 71; digit 4 → I-can 91 / 9; 5 → I-can 77; 0 (XLP / XFUN) → I-can). Per MODULE the gold is consistent (only 6 of ~130 modules mix both forms: BLL240, ENGI103, ENGI302, ENGJ402, ENGS302, ENGS401), so a per-module year table would work, a per-series one would not. **Consequence for the round: the wording-normalisation half falls back to the writer's own label on EVERY module (01B's "level unknown" clause, as Chris's answer allows) unless a year table arrives as data (`Module_Structure_Index.module_meta.year` or a KB file — a KB / data session, NOT something to ask Chris again; ship the hook, fill it later).** What CAN be normalised without a year: the label's FORM (trailing colon, sentence case) — measure the gold's exact strings first.
  (b) **The gold keeps a section title on 257 of 1,056 lesson pages** (`h5` "learning intentions" 161, `h5` "how will i know if i've learned it?" 126, `h3` 63 / 40, `h4` 32 / 19 …) — dropping them per 01B 223 is a NAMED override on those 257 pages (Chris's Option A accepts it; record per page).
  (c) **The ROLE classifier's input (Claude's lead-ins in the menu, with the gold's tag for the same label):** success `you will show your …` 367 (gold h5 195 / absent 116 / p 28 / h3 15 / h4 13), learning `we are learning` 290 (h5 195 / p 86), success `i can` 277 (h5 151 / p 98 / absent 18 / h3 10), learning `we are learning to` 236 (h5 151 / p 50 / h4 14 / h3 12), `learning intentions` 130 (h5 76 / h3 27 / absent 19 / h4 8), `we are learning about` 115 (h5 71 / p 29 / h3 13), success `you will be able to` 22 (gold p 22 — the c70-style sentence, NOT a label: exclude), `how will i know` 16 (h3 8 / absent 8), the OSSC `ākonga can / will …` family 13 + 13 + 9 + 7 + 4 + 3 + 2 (gold p / absent — the c70 lead-in sentence, stays `<p>`), `success criteria` 6 (h4 5). The classifier: a menu leaf whose folded text starts with a LEARNING lead-in (`we are learning`, `learning intention(s)`, `what are we learning`, `students will`, `i am learning to`, `walt`) or a SUCCESS lead-in (`i can`, `you will show / demonstrate your understanding`, `success criteria`, `how will i know`, `wilf`) is a label; `ākonga will / can …` and `you will be able to …` are sentences (c70) and stay `<p>`. The "absent" gold column (116 + 19 + 18 …) = pages where the gold has no such label at all (the human dropped or rewrote the menu) — named, not chased.

  aggregates, row 8 the empty hand-off boxes (sized r325: no single shape ≥ 20), row 12 over-capture (42 pages, the r310 class) — and **row 15,
  the literal-tag LEAK (26 modules / 46 pages / 288 occurrences), a PROTECTED GATE that has sat at 288/46 through every round and never had a
  PICK**; (c) KB §D has no NOT CAPTURED row ≥ 20 pages that is not BLOCKED or DECLINED.
- **Class:** a writer's structural tag typed in a NON-STANDARD RED ships as literal text. The engine scans ONLY red runs for tags
  (`Input_Doc_Rules.red_runs.red_hex_values` = `ff0000` / `ee0000`; TagNormaliser's contract: "takes one raw span of writer text (a red span)";
  `ListsAndRuns.#renderBlackText`'s r73 note: "only RED text is scanned for [tags] … a black one … LEAKS through as literal content"). The HIS
  NCEA1 writer's red is `ed0000`, ENGFUN02's is `fa0000`, the OS* / CED / ARFUN / TEFUN writers use Word's standard "Dark Red" `c00000` — so
  `[H3] Using browser controls…` ships as `<p>[H3] Using browser controls…</p>` while the same module's `ff0000` tags convert.
- **Measured corpus-wide (`_measure_r342_blacktags.py` → `_r342_blacktags.{json,log}`, every bracket group in every WT that folds to a
  Tag_Lexicon alias, tallied by the colour of the run holding its `[`):** 96,817 red tag brackets vs **1,169 non-red**: NEAR-RED (hue red,
  r ≥ B0 / g,b ≤ 40, not on the list) **598** — `ed0000` 311 · `fa0000` 165 · `c00000` 120 · `ed1c24` 1 · `f72b2b` 1 — on **30 modules (28 gated)**:
  Standard 17 (HIS1005 97, HIS1002 83, CEDO501 27, HIS1006 15, OSGM301/401/501 + OSAH501 + OSOH301/401 32, HIS1001 3, XDLS904/905/912, ENGS101,
  ENGI102, PES1008), Bilingual 8 (TRR102 72, TRR111 20, TRR103/106/109/112/113 33), Fundamentals 3 (ARFUN05 15, TEFUN03 10, TEFUN05), Inquiry 2
  (ENGFUN02 164, CEDK401 5); link-BLUE 250 (`1155cc` / `3494ba` / `0563c1` — the BLL writers' hyperlinked `[audio N]` tags, 3 of the 5 heaviest
  modules have no Claude dir); plain BLACK ≈ 300 (`auto` 192, `000000` 101 — HIS1002's `[LESSON 1]` / `[End page]` boundaries, XDLS902's
  `[Video url]` ×13, TRR109's `[H1]`/`[H2]` ×15). Position: 887 paragraph-leading / 282 mid-paragraph. Gate view: the 288 counted leaks sit
  on 46 pages; ~137 Claude pages in the 28 near-red modules still carry a literal tag somewhere (~65 outside placeholders / notes).
- **The RISK measured (`_measure_r342_nearred_content.py` → `_r342_nearred_content.log`): near-red runs that hold NO bracket** — `ed0000` 170
  paragraphs (HIS1002 126 = the writer's drag-and-drop label lists and instructions, HIS1005 10 = "Please place this original image…", HIS1006
  10 = "Lesson 1 (1.0)" menu lines, TRR102 23 = answer keys / "Audio Files"), `c00000` 46 — and `c00000` is CONTENT in MXFU302 ("+ 5 = 9" ×7),
  ENGI401 ("tone" / "pace" ×5), ENGS301, MXEX401, CEDO501's own word list ("Internet" / "Website"), OSGM301/501's option lines. A blanket
  "near-red = red" rule would turn that content into stripped red-text instructions → NOT the rule. **The discriminator: a near-red run that
  CARRIES A BRACKET.** Every one of the 598 bracket-bearing near-red runs resolves to a lexicon tag; no bracket-less run is touched, no run
  that is red today changes — strictly additive.

  gold: the human renders every near-red tag as its element on every triangulated site (the writer's tag is the writer's tag). Class B-i

  (nearRedOn && hueRed(color) && /[\[\]]/.test(runText))`. The exact `red_hex_values` list stays the primary rule (untouched).

## Session 7 · Round 3 (engine r340) — what shipped (a standalone [link] paragraph that is nothing but a video url is the embedded video) — FINISHED IN SESSION 8
- **Fix:** `elements.external_link_video_embed` {enabled, env LINKVID_OFF, host_match, media_follow} + `MediaBuilder.LinkVideoEmbedOn / LinkVideoHost /
  LinkVideoUrlOnly / FollowingVideoLinkTag`. **Seam A** (`ContentConverter.#inline`, before the r76 standalone-button rule): an `[external link]`-
  family item that is url-only — `!labelText` AND the item's WHOLE paragraph block minus red spans / urls / `*` is empty — whose url is a video
  url → `MediaBuilder.media(it, [it], 0, "video", run)`. **Seam B** (`MediaBuilder.media`'s following-link source): a `[video]` / `[embed]`
  element with no own url whose next unconsumed item is such a url-only external-link TAG item takes that url and consumes the item (the r247
  black-line rule extended to the tagged line); the `[embed]` route peeks the same helper so the phantom "[video] with no URL found" note never
  prints. `host_match` falls back to r339's `buttons.video_destination.host_match`.
- **The fence the probe forced (a narrowing, not a revert):** the first 416-module probe changed 22 pages / 12 modules — HPRE203_5_0, TEFUN07_0_0,
  XDLS908_5_0 were the PROSE form (18–31 visible words BEFORE a trailing `[link] URL`; gold ANCHOR), reached because `blackAfter` sees only the
  text after the tag. `LinkVideoUrlOnly` applies the measurement's whole-paragraph `words 0` definition on both seams → the second probe = 19 / 9.
- **Regeneration:** scoped — the in-memory OFF/ON probe over ALL 416 modules: OFF = disk 2102/2102; ON = exactly 19 pages / 9 modules
  (`_r340_affected.txt`: ANZH303 ENFUN01 HIS1005 HIS1006 HIS1007 HIS1008 MXDI202 MXFL203 MXFU202); planner batches (3) all rc 0; 0 truly stale;
  manifest diff = exactly the 19 / 9; disk == probe ON on all 88 pages of the 9. Word-loss over the 19 (`_r340_wordloss.py`, OFF vs disk): 0
  non-video links lost, only the 27 dropped "go to video" labels. The PICK's other 3 modules are unreachable by these seams and recorded: EXBP901 /

## Session 22 — Round 1 PICK (engine r369): the page's activity numbers are consecutive — a duplicate writer id, and a foreign lesson digit on a lesson page, take the gold's consecutive form
- **Class.** On a lesson page, Claude ships every writer id that carries a letter VERBATIM (the round-88 rule in `ActivitiesBuilder.activityOpen`: only a bare digit is renumbered `{lessonNumber}{letter}`; a lettered id is kept "because it can encode the true position when the capture is incomplete"). So a page ships `5A, 5A` (two writer boxes with the same id — AGH1001 L5, AGH1009 L5 / L7), `1A, 1A, 1B` (ANZH301 L1), `4A, 4B, 2A` (AGH1007 L4 — a journal-instruction box carrying the writer's stale digit), `1A, 1B, 1C, 1C` (ANZH101 L1). The gold numbers them consecutively: `5A 5B`, `1A 1B 1C`, `4A 4B 4C`, `1A 1B 1C 1D`.
- **Authority (§1b): 1 — the KB.** 00B_CONVERSION_PIPELINE line 187 + constraint 62 (00E): "the first interactive keeps the writer's activity number; each subsequent interactive takes the next letter … renumber the following activities accordingly"; constraint 65 / 05D: the MTK quiz box "carries the next consecutive activity number even where the writer assigned none". The gold agrees (3): Standard lesson pages with lettered boxes follow `{page number}{A, B, C …}` exactly on 1327 / 1540 = **0.86** (`_s22_actnum3.py`); Inquiry 47 / 50; the round-306 note ("the writer's own id, never invented; the gold's renumber is a NAMED divergence") predates §1b and is not a Chris decision (no D10 row covers ids) — it stands for a writer's GAP letters (kept), not for duplicates and foreign digits.
- **Measured before coding (`outputs/_s22_actnum5.py` → `_s22_actnum5.log` / `.json`, over the gate's own pairs, box-by-box in page order).** Variant DL = majority digit on the page (≥ 2 boxes, a strict majority; lesson pages only) + a duplicate `(digit, letter)` takes the next unused letter for its digit: **Standard** 1186 pairs with boxes on both sides — pages whose whole number list matches the gold 510 → 556 (**47 gained / 1 lost** — BLL152 L2, the gold keeps a writer's `1E`), boxes agreeing position-wise 2851 → 3079 (+228 of 3724); **Inquiry** 562 → 582 boxes, no page lost; **Bilingual** untouched (the decimal MTK ids never enter); **Fundamentals** single-page / phase pages EXCLUDED from the digit part (lessonNumber null — the r325 phase ordinal owns those digits; with the digit part on they lost 11 boxes), the letter part alone there +5 boxes / 1 page gained / 0 lost. Claude pages the rule changes: Standard 262 / 128 modules, Inquiry 14 / 10, Fundamentals (letters only) 7 / 7 → ≈ 283 pages / 145 modules. Structure-only (derivable by construction); above the 20-page floor in Standard and Inquiry.
- **Fix (DATA OVER CODE).** One page-level post-pass at the OUTERMOST of ContentConverter's body chain (after `#cdTilePair`, whose tile pairing reads the writer ids): `Emit_Templates.activity_wrapper.page_number_normalise` `{ enabled, env: "NUMNORM_OFF", majority_digit: true, dedupe_letters: true }`. Nothing else in the box moves; a writer's gap letters (`9A, 9B, 9D`) stay; ids with a dot (the KB 07B decimal family) and bare digits are never touched; an override-carrying box (`data-` / tile pairing) keeps its id because the pass runs after every consumer.
- **Regeneration (§0a / §0b):** the `number=` attribute is emitted for every activity box → the family is the whole corpus; a FULL regeneration at the §10a cadence (the ledger is FULL, counter 0 since r366 — allowed).
- Triangulation: AGH1007 (WT `[Button] Complete activity 2A …` on Lesson 4 → gold `4A 4B 4C` → Claude `4A 4B 2A`), AGH1001 L5 (two writer `[Activity 5A]` → gold `5A 5B` → Claude `5A 5A`), ANZH301 L1 (gold `1A 1B 1C` → Claude `1A 1A 1B`).

## Session 23 — Round 1 PICK (engine r370) + what shipped (archived from LOOP_STATE.md 2026-09-18 ≈09:55 NZST, session 23)

## Session 23 — Round 1 PICK (engine r370): the combined Writers Template's MEDIA-LIST PREAMBLE leaks into the last lesson page — written before any code, 2026-09-18 09:20 NZST
- **How it was found (not the miner's queue — a second lens).** The session-22 exhaustion verdict was TESTED, not rubber-stamped, with three instruments the miner does not have (`outputs/_s23_gap_rank.py` — pages ranked by ceiling-minus-score; `_s23_textcluster.py` — every differing line keyed by its TEXT; `_s23_button_census.py` / `_s23_journal_census.py` / `_s23_lessonsummary.py` / `_s23_addbutton.py` / `_s23_pairing_census.py`). The text-cluster lens surfaced a 12-module `EXTRA body li>a «if a specific third-party item/image is crucial …»` row (below the miner's floor because the gate pairs only 12 of the pages); the corpus-wide grep behind it: **136 Claude lesson pages / 134 modules** (Standard 85 / Fundamentals 27 / Inquiry 24) ship the Writers Template's media-list instruction bullet — "If a specific third-party item/image is crucial to your writing, then please submit this for early copyright clearance now/before starting writing [Early copyright review.docx]" — as learner-facing body text; **the gold ships it on 0 pages**; 435 WTs carry it.
- **Mechanism (triangulated BLL142 / BLL144 / ANZH203 — WT → gold → Claude).** In a COMBINED `Writers Template + Media List.docx` the media-list section opens after the last content page with a red `MEDIA LIST` heading, the red instruction "Please supply details for ALL external media …", the plain-text copyright bullet (with its sharepoint link), then the media TABLE. `ModuleResolver.PrepareRun` excludes only the TABLE block (`run.wtBlocks = TrimFrontMatter(…).filter(b => b !== mediaTable.block)`); the preamble blocks stay in the content stream: the two red runs become `Writers Note:` cv2-notes (gate-excluded chrome), the black bullet renders as `<ul><li>…<a>` on the last page. The AR-5 trailing-boilerplate rule (`PageSplitter`, after the final `[end page]`) cannot catch it: on the 134 WTs the tag before the preamble is `[body]` / `[button]` / `[image]` / a bare `[End]` (a CONTAINER_CLOSE alias, not a page boundary) — 16 have no tag within 8 lines at all.
- **Class.** Structure-only (a drop): derivable 1.00; gold consensus 1.00 in every template group (0 of 2385 gold pages carry it); pages 136 ≥ the 20-page body floor; modules 134. Authority: KB level 1 — `Input_Doc_Rules.json` `_meta.purpose` ("which parts of the Writers Template + Media List are real content, which are boilerplate to discard … a new boilerplate block = a data edit HERE") and the KB's media-list guidance (01C: the media table is data for acknowledgements, never page content). Not a gold-matching class: a template-boilerplate LEAK class (the r341 literal-leak family). Gate-visible on the 12 paired pages (EXTRA `ul` / `li` / `a` lines removed); the other 124 pages are unpaired or the bullet sits in a WIDGET-collapsed subtree — the value is learner-facing, the gate holds.
- **Fix (DATA OVER CODE).** `Input_Doc_Rules.json` gains `media_list_preamble` (enabled, env `MLPREAMBLE_OFF`, a phrase list: `media list`, `please supply details for all external media`, `third-party item/image is crucial`, `early copyright`, `add additional rows if necessary`); `DocxExtractor.TrimMediaListPreamble(blocks, tableBlock, run)` — for a COMBINED doc only (the table block is inside the WT's own blocks), walk BACKWARDS from the media table while each block is blank or matches a preamble phrase (folded, red markers stripped) and drop that run together with the table; one info note. A separate Media List docx is untouched by construction. `ModuleResolver.PrepareRun` calls it in place of the bare `.filter`.
- **Probe plan.** In-memory A/B over all 416 modules (`_ab.py MLPREAMBLE_OFF=1`), the ON pages scored with `_skeleton_compare.match()` BEFORE any regeneration (the r367 / r369 lesson); expected: 136 pages change, every change a removed `ul/li/a` (+ the two notes), no page loses content the gold has. Then a SCOPED regeneration of the 134 modules (the class is the media-list tail — its family IS the affected set), `run_all_gates.sh`, finalise.
- **Also recorded this session (no round).** (a) The journal-button FORM: the gold is era-split — `a[href=drive.google.com/…/my-drive] > div.button "Go to journal"` on 573 pages (the older series: AGH10 / HIS10 / PES10 / PHE10 / MXFL / MXDB / ENGI10-20 … 1.00) vs `h4.goJournal` on 263 pages (the newest series: ENGI30/40, ENGC30, ENGJ30, ENGR30, ENGS30, TEDC40, XGF90, ANZH30 — 1.00); Claude ships the h4 = the r239 developer-feedback FORWARD-LOOKING override (`Subject_Global_Parameters._universal_conventions.r239_go_journal_h4`), which the per-series split CONFIRMS as the current practice — not a class; the 1,170 WT journal tags in 121 modules map ≈ 1:1 onto the gold's 1,272 elements, so only the 33 modules with a gold button and NO tag (232 buttons from plain-text mentions) remain the needs-Chris question. (b) The literal `[Add button] <label>` paragraphs (61 / 28 pages / 9 modules): every one sits inside a `cv2-int-raw` hand-off dump — the D10-3 verbatim member dump, by design; not a leak. Every other candidate row carries its session 21–22 disposition (the STOPPED entries above). Every carried candidate row was dispositioned in sessions 21–22 (the STOPPED entries) and the heading-level class is not a queue row — the miner keys a heading line by its level, so a `[H3]` the gold ships at h3 and Claude at h4 lands in the generic "heading level differs" fragments below the floor; the class was found by the session-23 full-population writer-heading census (`outputs/_s23_headcensus.py` → `_s23_headcensus.{json,log}`: every explicit `[H1]`–`[H5]` in a paired module's WT matched by text on BOTH the gold and the Claude page).

The gold ships `<h3>`. Claude ships `<h4>` (or `<h5>`): the r45 `logical_to_element.body_shift: 1` makes the writer's digit an INTERMEDIATE h4, then `#relevelHeadings` ranks the page's distinct free-body levels from `base_level` 3 — so a `[H3]` sits at h3 only when it is the page's top level; on any page that also carries a `[H2]` (rank 1 → h3) it ranks second → h4, and under a `[H2]` + a page-title `[H1]` → h5. The gold does not rank: it keeps the writer's `3`.
- **Class (the census, n = 2549 explicit `[H3]` matched on both pages).** Gold keeps the digit **0.87** overall, Claude 0.59. By template, FREE-body (not inside an alert-family box): **Standard n=2054 — gold-h3 0.90; pinning to h3 = 569 win / 103 loss / 1287 already right / 95 neutral**; **Fundamentals n=314 — 0.86; 188 / 14 / 82 / 30**; Inquiry n=114 — 0.62, 36 / 31 = a near-tie → EXCLUDED; ConnectED 55 / 54 → excluded; Bilingual → excluded (KB 07B governs the MTK section heading). INSIDE a callout (`div.alert…`): gold-h3 **0.20** (Standard 2 win / 51 loss) — the KB 05B alert form puts the alert's title at h4 → EXCLUDED. The other digits: `[H2]` gold-keeps 0.37 (net −61 under a pin), `[H4]` 0.51 (a tie, net −42), `[H5]` 0.72 (+16, too small) — only digit 3 qualifies. Win / loss counted the honest way (a pin is a LOSS wherever the gold and Claude already agree on a non-h3 level — 117 `h4 = h4` + 53 sites in Standard / Fundamentals). Pages touched (Standard + Fundamentals, free, win or loss): **220 pages / 115 modules** ≥ the 20-page body floor; share 0.90 / 0.86 ≥ 0.60. - **Fix (DATA OVER CODE).** `Emit_Templates.body_region.heading_relevel.keep_writer_digit` {`enabled`, `env: "H3KEEP_OFF"`, `digits: [3]`, `templates: ["Standard", "Fundamentals"]` (matched against `Module_Structure_Index.module_meta[code].template_type` — the r364 precedent; a module the index does not know is not pinned), `exclude_classes: ["alert", "alertActivity", "important", "whakatauki", "wananga", "quoteText"]` (a heading inside such a box keeps the rank rule)}. Engine: `ContentConverter.#element`'s heading emit (the `<h${shifted}>` at the `#stripHeadingItalic` line) adds a TRANSIENT marker `data-wd="3"` when the writer's digit is listed, the template qualifies and re-levelling is on; `#relevelHeadings` treats a marked heading like a fixed-level one — excluded from the rank pool, rewritten to `<h3>` (its close tag too) — unless an `exclude_classes` box or a widget / supervisor subtree is open around it (then the marker is dropped and the standing rule applies; an ACTIVITY subtree keeps the pin — the census's gold-h3 share includes the box-internal `[H3]`s); the marker never reaches the output (stripped in every branch, incl. `RELEVEL_OFF` — the emitter only writes it when the re-leveller is on). OFF (`H3KEEP_OFF`) = no marker = byte-identical (the r370 shape of proof).

Ship only if the scorer agrees (net pp-sum positive, the dips named); else decline with the numbers. Then the SCOPED regeneration of the changed modules (the probe proving the rest byte-identical), `run_all_gates.sh`, finalise, commit.
- **What shipped (r371, build 260619.42, ≈10:20 NZST).** The PICK's fix with two refinements the probe forced: (1) THE POOL — the first cut took a pinned heading OUT of the rank pool and the scorer read 179 up / 105 down (+167.7) with the dips on pages that had been right (with the `[H3]` gone from the pool the page's `[H4]`s rose to h3 — OSAI401 −7.0, MXEO201 −11.5, MXEO401 −13.6); the pinned heading now still COUNTS in the pool at its intermediate level, so no other heading re-ranks; (2) `exclude_code_prefixes` ENG (gold-h3 0.05, 1 win / 14 loss) and ARFUN (0.36) whose gold follows the shift, CEDO (8 / 24) and XGF (31 / 42) net-negative. ## Session 24 — Round 6 PICK (engine r382): THE INSTRUMENT — the skeleton reader's body-element blind spot (a measurement-tool round, the r190 / r315 / r355 class)

- **Class (the gate, not the converter):** `_structural_skeleton.skeleton()` isolates the page with `<body …>(.*)</body>`; when that regex fails it parses the WHOLE document. **99 gold pages have NO `<body>` element at all** (the CED NCEA family — CEDO501 15, CEDT501 15, CEDO502 13, CEDK501 11, CEDW501 11 — ENGR301 9, ENGR202 8, MXEO401 6, XTAS101 6, MXFU401 5: `<div id="header">` sits directly under `<html>`), so their skeleton carries `html`, `head`, `meta ×3`, `title` before the content; **BLL144-1.0 has a `<body>` with no close tag**, so its content sits one indentation level deeper than Claude's and matches nothing (4.6 %). The DIFF MINER's `page_lines` has the same idiom.
- **Measured (a patched reader over the 96 paired pages, `outputs/_s24_r382_measure.log`):** 96 up / 0 down, pp-sum **+313.1** → corpus mean **+0.160pp** — an INSTRUMENT correction, never a gain (the r355 rule): BLL144-1.0 4.6 → 57.1, CEDT501_4.0 76.4 → 86.3, ENGR301_0.0 71.0 → 79.3, MXEO401_0.0 62.5 → 70.0 … The corpus itself does not change (no engine edit, no regeneration).
- **Fix:** one helper `_structural_skeleton.body_source(html)` shared by `skeleton()` and `_diff_miner.page_lines`: the closed body as before; an unclosed `<body …>` → from its open tag to the end; no body at all → the content after `</head>` wrapped in a bare `<body>` (no class is invented — the gold has no such element; the one line honestly mismatches Claude's `body.container-fluid`). `--selftest` gains the two cases (a body-less page and an unclosed body score as the closed page does). Re-baseline: the full skeleton score re-established on the r381 corpus, the miner re-run, `gate_baseline.json` + CLAUDE.md §9 + the changelog record it as an instrument correction; AppVersion 260619.53 (the r355 precedent — a tooling round still bumps).
- **Plan:** implement → selftest → full skeleton `--json` → `run_all_gates.sh` → miner re-run → finalise → state → commit.
**What shipped (r382, build 260619.53 — gate tooling only):** `_structural_skeleton.py` gains `body_source(html)` (a closed `<body>…</body>`; an unclosed `<body>` to the document's end with the trailing `</html>` dropped; a body-less page = the content after `</head>` in a BARE `<body>` — no class invented) and `skeleton()` calls it; `_diff_miner.page_lines()` calls the same reader; `_skeleton_compare.py --selftest` gains the two cases (body-less / unclosed-body must score as the closed page — 0.71 / 0.00 under the old reader, 1.00 under the new). Measured on the r381 corpus (`_s24_r382_sk_final.json`, `_s24_gatecmp.py _s24_r381 _s24_r382`): 96 movers, ALL up, 0 down, pp-sum +188.3 (BLL144_1_0 4.6→57.1; CEDT501_4_0 +6.0; the CED pages mostly +0.2…+1.5); skeleton 53.1229→53.2192 (+0.096pp), ≥50 1143→1148, ≥75 183→184, ≥90 15, RAW 37.492→37.541; every other gate EXACT (`_s24_r382_gates.log`). The pre-measure's +0.160pp (a monkeypatched scorer) over-read it — the shipped full run is the figure. The miner re-mined under the same reader: 8352 classes / 177 CANDIDATE, none added or removed (`root` lines 1892→1409; title row #5 11→10 pages; the root row `html.notranslate`→`body`, 94 pp / 10 mods, DECLINED as before — a gold-side omission); pre-r382 queue kept at `outputs/_diff_queue_pre_r382.md`. No shape discriminator (columns, rows, HTML bold, the WT's `**bold**`) separates the gold's th from its td — EXCEPT the cell length: a first row with a cell of **≥ 9 words** is `td` **0.88** gold-wide (n=141) and **0.92** on the 25 Claude tables (22 pages) that pair with a gold table by first-cell text; every shorter band stays th 0.77–0.84 paired (Claude's rule is right there). Probe OFF = disk 2109/2109; ON in memory over all 416 → pagescore BEFORE regen; word-bag; scoped regen of the changed modules (the table family is 100 modules — under the §10a 60 % guard); gates; finalise.
- **Not taken:** the writer's bold first row as a th signal (gold th 0.86 on 56 matched sites, but the plain short row is ALSO th 0.60 — the default already covers both); the 5–8-word band (paired th 0.84 — stays a header).

The demoted row renders through `cell` (td) with `renderCellInline`'s isHeader false (a `[Body, bold]` cell keeps its `<b>`). - **The instrument (NEW — the reason a class exists after session 24's exhaustion):** `outputs/_s25_rowcol.py` (the ROW / COLUMN COMPOSITION census of `#body > div.row > div.col-*`, gold vs Claude, per template / subject / page type — the r51 row-grouping rule was mined on 370 pages in June and never re-measured at the column level) → `_s25_rowcol.out`; `_s25_headpos.py` (WHERE every h2–h5 and alert SITS: row-open / col-open / inline-after-content / nested) → `_s25_headpos.out`; `_s25_h3pair.py` + `_s25_h3notgold.py` (each Claude row-opening h3 paired to the gold's placement of the same text) → `_s25_h3pair_h3.out`, `_s25_h3notgold.out`; `_s25_widgetrow.py` (how a built widget sits, by type) → `_s25_widgetrow.out`; `_s25_h45flow.py` (the simulation on the gate's own scorer) → `_s25_h45flow_{h5,h4,pick}.out/.json`.
- **What the census found (Standard, 1738 paired pages):** Claude ships 12,110 top-level body rows to the gold's 10,497 (+15 %). The gold's single-column row IS the convention (0.82 — multi-column rows are the r331 sidebars, not a class) and a heading DOES open a row in the gold (h3 row-open 0.80 of column-level h3s — the r51 rule holds). The surplus rows: h3 +933 (the gold's same-text heading is row-open 0.40 / NOWHERE on the gold page 0.26 = the writer's own `[H3]` / `[H2]` / `[H1]` tags the human dropped or reworded, 363 of 570 — class C / nested inside an activity 0.08 = the box-membership family already worked / inline after content 0.04), alert +390 (the gold's non-sidebar alert opens a row at 0.64 — the r51 rule holds; ENGI4 0.60 n=20 is the only ≥ 0.60 flow group, under the floor), **h4 +387 and h5 +152 — the MINOR headings: the gold flows an h5 inline after content at 0.76 (Standard; lesson pages 1.00, Mathematics 0.88, English 0.67, NCEA1 1.00) and an h4 at 0.50 corpus-wide = a TIE that splits by subject: NCEA1 0.79 (n=105), Leaving to Learn 0.66 (62), 1-10 Mathematics 0.64 (136) vs English 0.25, Online Safety 0.30, ConnectED 0.36; ANZH 0.70 (n=10) and 'None' 0.64 (11) under the floor.** Claude breaks a row at EVERY heading level (`ContentConverter.rowFor("heading")`, the r51 `row_breaks.flow_blocks` rule, no level or family distinction).
- **The class:** an h5 anywhere in the Standard template, and an h4 in the subject groups {NCEA1, 1-10 Mathematics, Leaving to Learn}, joins the open section row (no `breakRow()`); a heading that follows a closed activity / callout / widget still opens a fresh row (`row_breaks.after` unchanged); h2 / h3 unchanged everywhere; Inquiry / Fundamentals / Bilingual untouched (their h5s are the panel labels; Inquiry h5 6 up / 0 down in the simulation but the census has < 15 column-level h5s there — recorded below floor).
- **Measured on the gate's own scorer before any code (`_s25_h45flow.py pick`, the skeleton lines merged as the rule would):** Standard 141 pages touched (432 candidate rows, 312 merged), **106 up / 29 down / 6 flat, pp-sum +29.0** → ≈ +0.015pp corpus mean; NCEA1 39 pp 32 up / 6 down +13.4; Mathematics 41 pp 34 / 6 +10.6; Leaving to Learn 28 pp 18 / 9 +3.7; English (h5 only) 21 pp 12 / 7 ±0.0. The declined half: h4 corpus-wide is NET NEGATIVE (−45.5; English −38.6, Online Safety −17.3, ConnectED −17.5, CEDO5 −18.2) — a tie is not a rule; h3 flow (gold 0.16) not a candidate.
- **KB-first check:** no KB rule on how free-body sections group into top-level rows (02B / 05B / CL-0077 govern the INNER rows of activities and alerts only) → authority §1b level 3 (the module's own gold, group consensus ≥ 0.60) / level 4 (the subject consensus).
- **Implementation plan:** data `Emit_Templates.json body_region.row_breaks.flow_minor_headings` {enabled, levels_everywhere: [5], levels_by_subject: {4: [NCEA1, 1-10 Mathematics, Leaving to Learn]}, templates: [Standard]} + env `MINORHEADFLOW_OFF`; `rowFor("heading")` receives the level and the page's subject / template and returns without `breakRow()` when the level flows. Scoped regeneration (§0a: the h4 / h5 family = every module whose WT carries a `[H4]` / `[H5]` or a relevelled minor heading — resolved by the probe's ON list) = scoped ship #7 since the r377 FULL (the backstop is due at 8).

- **VERDICT — DECLINED on measurement, 2026-09-18 ≈20:15 NZST (no code written, no regeneration; §3 step 3).** The gold-side census's "inline" majority does not survive PAIRING: `_s25_h3pair.py` pairs every Claude row-opening minor heading to the gold's rendering of the same text. Standard/lesson h5 (165): 71 have NO gold heading of that text (the gold nests the writer's `[H5]` in an accordion / relevels it — ENGJ402 L7 `[H5] Similarities` → gold `<h3>` opening a row; ENG1005's overview h5s inside a gold accordion), and among the paired ones the gold FLOWS 33 vs OPENS A ROW 25 = **0.57, a tie** on ≈ 25 pages. Standard/lesson h4 (726): the paired gold opens a row 198 (h4 127 + h3 71) vs flows 75 = **0.73 row-open — the r51 rule is RIGHT**; NCEA1/lesson flows 21 vs 12 (0.64) on ≤ 20 pages — under the floor; Mathematics and Leaving to Learn row-open. The `_s25_h45flow.py` "+29.0 pp-sum" was the wrapper-removal artefact: deleting Claude's `div.row` + `div.col-12.col-md-8` above a heading the gold has nowhere at that position shortens the EXTRA run and lifts the difflib ratio without matching anything — not a structural gain (the r384 lesson: a probe scored on the gate is decisive only when the paired gold form agrees). Also confirmed on this corpus, all consistent with the engine: the gold's single-column row 0.82 (multi-column rows = the r331 sidebars); a heading opens a row 0.80 (h3, column-level); the `row_breaks.after` rule — an alert is its column's LAST child at 0.86, an activity 1.00, a widget 0.67 (Claude 0.62); a built widget's position by type matches the gold's own majority (in-col); the gold's 1,626 extra `<br>` are inside widgets (invisible to the gate); the ORDER GAP (`_s25_order.py`: position-free 60.6 % vs sequence 53.2 % = 7.4pp corpus, Fundamentals 14.3, Inquiry 9.9) is dominated by known families (duplicate writer ids — r369; box membership; the panel interiors). Inside the Fundamentals / Inquiry panels (`--panels`): Claude ships 299 h5 to the gold's 106 (Fundamentals) and 233 row-opening h5 to the gold's 0 (Inquiry) — the writer's digits shift one level further than the gold's (`[H2]` → gold h3 / Claude h4, `[H3]` → gold h4 / Claude h5 in ARFUN05), concentrated on 12 (Fundamentals) / 3 (Inquiry) overview pages — under the 20-page floor; the s23 census's Inquiry `[H3]` near-tie (36 / 31) stands.

## Session 25 — Round 2 (archived from LOOP_STATE.md 2026-09-18 20:31 NZST) — PICK pass (2026-09-18 20:20 → 20:30 NZST): THE LOSS LEDGER — every lost point of the skeleton mean attributed to a family; EXHAUSTION re-confirmed in percentage points, no round started
- **The instrument (NEW):** `outputs/_s25_losspp.py` (the gate's own difflib alignment, every unmatched line weighted 1 / (|gold| + |Claude|) so a line on a 40-line page counts 5× one on a 200-line page — the miner's line COUNTS turned into pp of the corpus mean) and `outputs/_s25_ledger.py` (every unmatched line assigned to a FAMILY: chrome · widget-line · editorial-gold (gold text NOWHERE on Claude's page) · editorial-claude (Claude text NOWHERE on the gold page) · container-shift (the text exists in another container) · merged-split · tag-swap · alignment-residue (the same pad+signature unmatched on both sides) · wrapper-of-<family> (a structure-only line takes the family of the unmatched text it wraps) · wrapper-empty (wraps no text — media, br, buttons, icons) · wrapper-form (its content MATCHED, only the wrapper's form differs — the residue a converter rule could own)). Plus `_s25_pwhere.py` (the largest single cell — Claude's EXTRA free `p`, 2.12pp — located on the gold page) and `_s25_swallow.py` (gold text held inside a Claude BUILT widget, by builder).
- **The ledger (1,963 pairs, total loss 47.94pp):** wrapper-empty **7.28** (the human's own images 0.41 free + 0.31 activity + 0.14 alert, the gold's `<br>` spacing 0.64, the journal `div.button` 0.34 (needs Chris), the video wrapper's per-series `icon` token 0.16 (r200), legacy `iframe.embed-responsive-item` 0.16 (KB-correct)) · chrome **5.74** (the D10-9 / 01B / r360 / r317 overrides and decided classes) · alignment-residue **5.58** + its wrappers 1.49 (= the 7.4pp order-gap ceiling of `_s25_order.py`) · editorial-gold **3.76** + wrappers 2.18 (the human's additions — the ceiling's no-source share) · editorial-claude **3.51** + wrappers 1.39 (writer text the human DROPPED or rewrote — AGH1001's dairy list commented out for a flow diagram; `Career Example:` / `Topographic Descriptions` `[H3]`s dropped — NOT in the 91.9 % ceiling, which counts only gold text without a WT source) · container-shift **4.62** + wrappers 2.40 (activity→widget 1.35 + free→widget 1.21 = the un-built hand-off box holding the writer's text, D10-3 — built widgets swallow only 0.27pp: accordion 0.11 / carousel 0.05 / flipCard 0.05, every key under the floor; activity↔free 0.71 / 0.69 = the box-membership tie mined to r381) · widget-line **2.10** + wrappers 1.44 (A1 / D10-3) · merged-split **2.01** + 0.85 (rewording) · tag-swap **1.15** + 0.46 (the heading-ladder ties) · **wrapper-form 1.33** (EXTRA `col-12.col-md-8` 0.21 + `div.row` 0.14 vs MISSING `div.row` 0.07 + `col-12` 0.06 + `col-12.col-md-8` 0.06 — the row-break question in BOTH directions, a tie — the Round-1 decline; `col-md-12` / `col-md-4.paddingLR` widths ≤ 0.04 each = the declined width classes).
- **Per template:** Standard 46.3pp (the same shape); Inquiry 62.8 and Fundamentals 55.1 (editorial-gold 6.4 / 4.1 — the human's own panel content; alignment 7.6 / 6.5); Bilingual 62.1 (editorial-claude 9.7 + editorial-gold 8.6 = the MTK rewrite, KB 07B).
- **Verdict:** the residue a converter rule could own without NEW evidence is wrapper-form 1.3 + tag-swap 1.6 + the box-membership tie ≈ 1.4 ≈ 4pp, every part of it a measured tie or a recorded decline; the rest is the human's editorial work in both directions (≈ 10.8pp + wrappers 3.6), the widget builds (≈ 6.1 incl. the hand-off boxes), chrome overrides (5.7), order (7.1) and media / spacing (7.3). §4 EXHAUSTION stands — now in pp, not only in the miner's row count. **Note for the report:** the honest ceiling is LOWER than 91.9 % — the ceiling instrument counts gold lines with no WT source (8.1 %) but not the writer text the human removed (editorial-claude ≈ 3.5pp + its wrappers 1.4pp); both are unclosable by any rule.

- **Before-position (`_s26_before2.py`):** the gold's whakatauki OPENS its column at 0.78 (Claude 1.00) — the break BEFORE it is right; only the break AFTER is wrong.
- **PAIRED (`_s26_whpair.py`, the s25 lesson):** for each Claude whakatauki row, the next row's first text located on the gold page — Standard FLOW 35 / BREAK 8 = **0.81** (35 pages / 35 modules); Online Safety 24 / 2 = 0.92; Leaving to Learn 4 / 1; ConnectED 3 / 0; Maths / English / Science 1 / 0 each; **NCEA1 1 / 3 = 0.25** (HIS1005 / 1006 / 1008 "The whakataukī chosen for this module…" opens a gold row; HPRE203) — EXCLUDED, consistent with its gold-side census 0.56 tie; Inquiry 0 / 1 and Bilingual (TMoA 5 / 5 LAST) — EXCLUDED; gold-nowhere 19 (the commentary the human dropped / rewrote — editorial, untouched by the fix); untouched 10 (a heading / wananga / widget / alert follows — those open a row regardless).

## Session 26 — Round 4 PICK (engine r390): THE SUPERVISOR NOTE'S EXPLICIT CLOSER — a `[Supervisor button] … [End Supervisor button]` / `[Supervisor note] … [end supervisor note]` pair is a SPAN: everything between is the panel's content
- **The lead:** the r388 paired row-break census, direction A, kind `row` = `div.row.super-content` (the non-activity supervisor panel): Claude breaks the row after the panel and the gold puts the next text INSIDE the panel — 27 / 6 = 0.82 overall, **Leaving to Learn 22 / 0 = 1.00 (22 pages)**. The panel-content census (`outputs/_s26_r390_supclose.py` → `.out`, paired per group): LtL in-panel 22 / free 0 (4 modules), English 4 / 2 (ENGR102's empty panels), ANZH 1 / 0; Mathematics 0 / 9, BLL 0 / 4, OS 0 / 1, ConnectED 0 / 1 (the gold agrees with Claude there).
- **The mechanism (triangulated on XDLS904 lesson 1 / XDLS502 lesson 3 — WT → gold → Claude):** the writer opens `[Supervisor Button]` (r170 promotes it to the `supervisor note` CONTAINER_OPEN), types paragraphs + bullets whose items END in an inline red `[link to XFUN06]` marker, and CLOSES with `[End Supervisor button]` (XDLS904 / 905 / 906: 21 pairs) or `[end supervisor note]` (XDLS502, XDLS901). The gold's panel holds everything to the closer (p, ul, p, ul …); Claude's own_row panel is ALWAYS STRICT (`#calloutOpen(…, spans = false)` — "emitted complete") so `MediaBuilder.gatherFollowing` stops at the first tag item (the bullet's inline `[link to …]`) and the rest ships as free rows. The live parser: `[End Supervisor button]` → `end supervisor button` CONTAINER_CLOSE, `[end supervisor note]` → `end supervisor note` CONTAINER_CLOSE. Corpus-wide the explicit supervisor closer exists ONLY in the LtL family (the WT census: XDLS904/905/906 21 + XDLS901 1 + XDLS502 1 + XTAS102 1); every other family's note has no closer → untouched by construction.
- **KB-first check:** 05B / 01F give the panel form (`row.supervisor > super-content-button > super-content row > row > col-12 + col-12`); nothing on a writer-closed span → §1b level 3 / 4. A writer's explicit closer is the r105 span rule the alert family already honours (`#explicitCloseAhead`) — structure-only, derivable.
- **Fix (DATA OVER CODE):** `callouts.by_tag."supervisor note".explicit_close_span {enabled, env: SUPSPAN_OFF}`: the own_row branch tests `#explicitCloseAhead(bodyItems, i, tag, stopAtActivity = true)` — the family set now also accepts the closer of a `tag_promote` source (`end supervisor button` for `supervisor note`), and an `[Activity]` opener ends the scan (an own-row span never swallows an activity) — and on a hit opens the panel in SPAN mode (`#calloutOpen(…, true)`, stack mode `span-own`): the loop renders the items between inside the panel's column (emit() opens no content row while the own-row span is open — `ownSpanClose`), autoClose treats it as a writer's span (a heading never closes it; a section marker / page boundary does), the closer pops it. OFF = the strict panel (the r389 output). Expected ON: the LtL pages (~24) only. Regeneration: SCOPED (the probe's ON list) = scoped ship #2 since the r388 full.
- **Not taken:** the r162 in-panel italic strip is not applied to the span's loop-rendered items (XDLS is in `strip_italic_subjects` — record if the probe shows an `<i>`); ENGR102's empty panels (4 / 2, no closer — a different mechanism: the note's text sits in a `[body]` after the opener); MXDI103's panel truncation (the gold panel continues past a black run — no closer, Maths 0 / 9 at the row boundary).

Re-measured (`_s27_r1_videoicon.py`, `_s27_r1_videoicon2.py`, `_s27_r1_videoicon3.py`): (a) in the registry's own modules the gold puts `icon` on the WIDGET-EMBEDDED videos at 0.95 (carousel 364 / 382, accordion 41 / 43, tabs 42 / 44, clickDrop 112 / 122, panel 111 / 120, modal 9 / 9) — the r200 note left them plain as a recorded follow-up, never done: 261 paired lines on 44 pages / 39 modules (259 in carousels — gate-INVISIBLE in the scaffold, RAW-visible); (b) the registry re-mined on today's gold: 18 series solidify at n ≥ 2 modules / share ≥ 0.85, each with ≥ 8 gold videos, 14 of them at 1.00 (ANZH20 31/0, CEDR20 26/1, CEDR30, ENGC20 12/0, ENGC30 19/2, ENGJ10 26/0, ENGJ20 10/0, ENGR10 31/0, ENGS20 22/0, MXDB20 24/0, MXDB30 40/0, MXDI10 37/6, MXEO20 32/0, MXEX20 12/0, MXEX30 9/0, MXFL30 51/0, MXFU30 10/0, XDLS91 12/0) and `NCEA1|Standard` at the subject|template level (401 / 94 = 0.81, n = 41) — with a NEW plain-series carve-out (a series at n ≥ 2 and share ≤ 0.40 stays plain inside an icon subject group: PHE10 0.15, HES10 0.48 → carved; the r200 design had no carve-out, which is why NCEA1 could not solidify then).
