By template: Standard (Maths / English / NCEA1 / ANZH series) and Inquiry (XDLS91). The alternatives measured and NOT taken: A (r200's own n ≥ 3 / 0.80 thresholds, no carve-out) +32 net — NCEA1's PHE / HES drag; A' (+ carve-out) +62; C (the §1b 0.60 bar at the subject level: adds Maths|Standard 0.74, ConnectED|Inquiry 0.62) +382 net but 137 newly wrong (79 % precision) — the subject-level 0.60 groups are ties by series (MXDI20 0.43, MXFU40 0.21, MXFL10 0.60) and are left for a series-level re-mine when their n reaches 2.

## Session 27 — Round 2 PICK (engine r399) + what shipped (archived from LOOP_STATE.md 2026-09-19 ≈10:45 NZST, session 27)
## Session 27 — Round 2 PICK (engine r399): THE WĀNANGA / TALANOA BOX IS THE KB'S CULTURAL ALERT — the writer's `[Wānanga/Talanoa box]` + one-cell table ships as `div.alert.cultural[layout=combined] > row > col-12 > p…`, not an empty `div.wananga` + a kept `<table>`

- **Class.** The CED Phase-5 writers (CEDK501 / CEDO501 / CEDO502 / CEDR501 / CEDT501 / CEDW501) type `[Wānanga/Talanoa box]` (24 + 20 spellings, plus `[Wananga box]` ×4, `[Talanoa/Wananga box]` ×2, the typos `[Wanana/…]` / `[Wananaga/…]`, and CEDT501's `[Banner - Wānanga/Talanoa box]` ×11) on its own line, followed by a ONE-CELL table holding the discussion prompt (the cell's lines " / "-joined; sometimes "• " bullets). The gold renders every one as the KB's Cultural Alert — `<div class="alert cultural" layout="combined"><div class="row"><div class="col-12"><p>…</p>…` (63 / 63 gold boxes are `layout="combined"`; bullets become a `<ul>`; the tail-form `[Banner - …] prompt` becomes the same box with the tail as its `<p>`). Claude today: the tag resolves to `wananga` CONTAINER_OPEN (the lexicon's `wananga` / `talanoa` / `cultural alert` aliases, `[…box]` embedded) but the strict gather finds no black text (the next item is a TABLE) → an EMPTY `<div class="wananga">` carrying a "Red Flag: Empty [wananga]" note (56 flags on 35 pages / 6 modules) + the writer's table rendered as a kept `table.table.table-bordered`; the `[Banner - …]` form resolves to `carousel` (the `banner` alias outranks the container) and opens a rotateBanner bundle that declines. Found by the paired callout-class census (`_s27_r2_alertclass.py`: the gold-only `alert cultural[layout=combined]` 58 boxes) → the writer tag traced by `_s27_r2_wananga.py` (69 tag sites / 7 modules) and generalised by `_s27_r2_callouttbl.py` (EVERY callout tag typed bare + a one-cell table, all 416 WTs: the idiom is exactly this class — `[wananga…]` 55 sites / 6 modules / 37 gold pages, gold `alert cultural` 50 = 0.91, `alert` 1 (CEDK501's overview explanatory box), 2 inside a gold accordion panel, 2 not found; the only other tag with the idiom is `[alert]` on TEDC402's page-layout tables, 7 sites, gold not found).
- **Size.** 35 Claude pages / 6 modules today (the 6 CED modules; CEDT501 adds 10 gold pages once the Banner form promotes); gold pages with the class 49. Per site Claude loses `div.wananga` (an empty div — its note is gate-excluded) + `div.table-responsive` / `table` / `tr` / `td` and gains `div.alert.cultural[layout=combined]` / `div.row` / `div.col-12` / N × `p` (or `ul` / `li`) — every gained line the gold's own. Expected ≈ +4 to +8 lines matched per site on pages of 40–120 skeleton lines → ≈ +3–6pp per page, ≈ +150 pp-sum, mean ≈ +0.08pp; ≥50 up 1–3.
- **Authority (§1b).** KB 05B "Cultural Alert (Wānanga / Talanoa)" — the exact form, `layout` ∈ {wananga, talanoa, combined}; KB 14A §14.4 ConnectED Phase 5 "Wānanga / talanoa boxes … Supplied designer note — **all alerts are combined unless otherwise specified**" + the same `alert cultural layout="combined"` snippet; KB 01D tag taxonomy `alert-wananga` / `alert-talanoa` / `alert-combined` → `alert_cultural_*`; KB 01F the emit forms. The gold agrees at level 2 (the CED5 series: 50 / 55 = 0.91, every module ≥ 0.85). No sibling contradicts (no other family uses the tag).
- **Fix (DATA OVER CODE).** (1) `Emit_Templates.callouts.by_tag.wananga.kb_form {enabled, env: WANANGA_OFF, open: '<div class="alert cultural" layout="combined">', close, wrap_content: true}` — `#calloutOpen` swaps the def's open / close / wrap when on (the legacy `<div class="wananga">` stays as the OFF form). (2) `callouts.by_tag.wananga.table_cell_content {enabled, env: WANANGA_OFF}` — in STRICT mode, a callout whose own gather is empty and whose next unconsumed item is a ONE-ROW ONE-CELL table takes that cell as its content through `TablesAndGrids.renderCellParts` (`<p>` per line, `<ul>` for "• " lines, the cell's own `[H*]` / `[image]` parts honoured) and marks the table `_consumed`; `#calloutWrapsStructured` yields to it (so the box does not span-wrap a kept table instead). (3) `Tag_Lexicon._meta.tag_promote` rule `{from: carousel, to: wananga, to_directive: CONTAINER_OPEN, when_match: '^\s*\[\s*banner\s*[-–—:]\s*w[āa]nanga', env: WANANGA_OFF}` — CEDT501's `[Banner - Wānanga/Talanoa box]` is the same box (its 10 gold pages); `[Rolling Banner - …]` (1 site, a 4-cell strip the gold dropped) is excluded by the anchor. ## Session 27 — Round 5 PICK (engine r400) + what shipped — the un-numbered activity opener takes the next positional letter (19 Sept ≈12:45 → 12:58 NZST)

- **The class (found by the position-free label census's EXTRA side, `div.activity` 529 lines / 265 pages / 115 modules — the gold ships 6 numberless boxes in 2385 pages):** Claude ships 629 activity boxes with NO `number` attribute on paired pages (`_s27_r5_numberless.py`) — the writer's bare `[Activity]` / `[Activity: Embedded]` / `[interactive] …` openers with no id (AGH1001_3_0's `[interactive] Drag and drop onto above picture.` → a plain `div.activity`; the gold's `activity interactive number="3A"`). The gold numbers EVERY box positionally (`{lesson}{letter}`); `ActivitiesBuilder.activityOpen` only counts and letters an opener that carries an id or the r217 synthetic `positionalId` — an id-less writer opener neither takes a letter nor advances the lesson's letter counter.
- **The measurement (`_s27_r5_posnum.py`, every paired lesson page where Claude and the gold ship the same number of top-level boxes — 76 of the 261 pages with a numberless box):** the positional k-th letter equals the gold's number on **69 / 101 = 0.68** (56 pages / 34 modules; NCEA1 0.65, Leaving to Learn 0.67, Mathematics 0.86, English 5 / 5); the misses are lesson-number offsets (AGH1006_3_0's gold is lesson 4) that no numbering fixes; Claude's own numbered boxes on the same pages match the gold 77 / 104. A numberless label matches no gold line whatever the alignment, so a positional letter can only gain where it is right; the r369 alignment hazard (a wrong number stealing a gold line) is measured by the probe before any regeneration.
- **The fix:** data `activity_wrapper.lesson_letter_number.unnumbered_positional {enabled, env ACTUNNUM_OFF}` — in `activityOpen` an opener with no writer id on a page whose lesson number is known (`#pageLessonNumber != null`, the r217 gate; the r325 phase pages included) takes the next positional letter and advances the counter, exactly as the r217 synthetic box does; writer-lettered ids untouched; overview / single-file pages without a phase number unchanged. OFF = today's output byte-for-byte.
- **Gate expectation:** skeleton up (the `div.activity[number=…]` line now matches on ≈ 0.65 of the boxes + the box subtree aligns); compare_structure / body_compare unaffected (the number is not in the wrapper chain); scored with the gate's own `match()` on the probe's ON pages before regenerating; the family = every module whose page carries a numberless box (the probe's changed set).
- **What shipped (r400, build 260619.71):** the probe OFF 2109 / 2109; ON 281 pages / 94 modules; scored on the ON pages 67 up / 13 down / 186 same (+229.9); the 94 regenerated (10 batches, all rc 0; `fresh --affected` 0 truly stale; probe ON == disk 795 / 795); skeleton 53.862 → 53.979 % (+0.118pp; 80 movers 67 up / 13 down, 0 outside the affected set — the 13 dips named in the changelog, the r369 letter-shift class: ENGI201_2_0 −11.3 the largest), ≥50 1170 → 1174, ≥75 197 → 200, ≥90 18, RAW 37.918 → 37.995 %; compare_structure 11798 / 172 / 626, body_compare 42 / 4 / 173 / 218, clean 2079 / 2102, leak 26 / 23 — EXACT; every verifier RESULT identical to r399; 15 selftests + the feature-index selftest GREEN; ledger scoped #4 since the r396 FULL; miner 173 → 169 CANDIDATE (four numberless-box rows gone, nothing new); checksums engine 4 changed / gates 0.

## Session 27 — Round 7 PICK (engine r402) + what shipped — the `[interactive: video]` line before a widget is the box's first lead element, not its swallowed opener (19 Sept ≈13:45 → 14:05 NZST)

- **The class (recorded at the r401 ship, AGH1005 lesson 2):** `[interactive: video]` / `[interactive: image]` / `[Interactive video]` parse to `primary = video / ELEMENT` with a second tag `activity` whose alias word is `interactive` (`_s27_r5_parse1.cjs`). `InteractiveScanner`'s owner lookback (`prev.parse.tags.some(t => t.tag === "activity")`) takes ANY span carrying an `activity` tag as the following widget's activity OWNER — so the writer's video line before `[interactive: drag and drop]` becomes the drag-and-drop's owner: the video never renders (the gold's `videoSection` is missing from Claude's page), the box opens with no title and (the bundle having captured nothing) holds only the `no content captured` flag.
- **To measure first (`_s27_r7_ownerscan.cjs`, the live scanner over all 416 modules):** every bundle whose `activityOwner`'s PRIMARY tag is not `activity` — by primary tag / directive and by the activity tag's alias word — and, for each, what the gold ships at that spot (a `videoSection` / `img` before the box, the box itself). The fix, if the population holds: the lookback accepts a span as the owner only when its activity tag is the span's primary or a bare / numbered `[Activity …]` form (a data list of the alias words that never open an activity — `interactive` — in `Interactive_Boundary_ChildTag_Bank._meta.opener_rule`), env `OWNERALIAS_OFF`; the media line then renders through the normal element path and the widget takes the r217 / r401 standalone form.
- **Gate expectation:** the media element restored on the affected pages (a `videoSection` / `img` line the gold has), the owner box replaced by the standalone box where the bundle has content or by the bare flag where it has none; scored on the probe's ON pages before regenerating.
- **What was measured (`_s27_r7_ownerscan.cjs`, the live scanner over all 416):** 128 owner spans whose primary is not `activity` (37 modules / 80 pages) — 108 carry the word `activity` itself (`[Activity 2A] [H3] Title` 78, `[Individual activity] [H3]`, `[Activity 5A: Video Review]` …) and are owners by right; **20 carry the `interactive` alias**: 14 `[interactive: video]` (AGH1004 / AGH1005 / AGH1006), `[interactive: Video – …]` TWHA902 / TWHK901 (video, `how = embedded`), and the embedded image / list instruction forms (AGH1009 ×2, ENFUN03, XTAS102).

- **Variant (b) — the span stays the owner but is the box's first LEAD element (a synthetic bare opener takes its place; the r364 lead_media path renders it): SHIPPED scoped** — unscoped 14 same / 4 down (the embedded image / list forms: AGH1009_8_0 −7.0 whose span's `3` had been the box id, ENFUN03 / XTAS102 / AGH1009_6_0 ≤ −0.2); with `element_tags` (video / audio / image) + `exclude_primary_hows` (embedded) the population is the 14 AGH `[interactive: video]` sites: probe OFF 2109 / 2109, ON 12 pages / 3 modules, every change a `cv2-note` (the video's `no URL` flag now surfaces inside the box, r203-coalesced with the widget's own flag where both print); scored 12 same; the 3 regenerated (1 batch rc 0; `fresh --affected` 0 truly stale; probe ON == disk 26 / 26); skeleton 53.989 / 1174 / 200 / 18 / RAW 38.000 page-for-page (0 movers); every other gate EXACT; every verifier RESULT identical to r401; 15 selftests + the feature-index selftest GREEN; ledger scoped #6 since the r396 FULL (2 of headroom); miner 169 → 169; checksums engine 3 changed / gates 0.
